# Bills sponsored by Malcolm Byrne: progress and outcomes

**Draft — for review** · Incomplete / qualified research

Original brief: Prepare a briefing on the Bills sponsored by Malcolm Byrne. What progress have they made, and what happened to them?
Reporting date: 2026-11-12

## Malcolm Byrne: sponsored Bills

The records give status labels and dated activity for 20 sponsored Bills, not certified outcomes: “Current” does not prove enactment, and “Lapsed” does not establish final fate. [a-55cc9116704f3354390c]
_Provisional interpretation — moderate qualitative confidence (provisional)_

Two records illustrate why entries should be read separately. Protection of Private Residences (Against Targeted Picketing) Bill 2021 has Fifth and Report Stage entries on 4 July 2024, a Bill Lapsed Event and Second Stage on 8 November 2024, and another Second Stage on 1 May 2025. Seanad Electoral (University Members) (Amendment) Bill 2020 has a Bill Lapsed Event and Committee Stage on 29 January 2025, followed by Committee Stage on 27 March 2025. The records do not reconcile these entries or certify final outcomes. [a-0cc01e609aea93ec3f2a]
_Provisional interpretation — moderate qualitative confidence (provisional)_
